# Live Page Verification Audit Report (`live-pages-audit.md`)

**Project:** EduHawk Study Abroad Website  
**Live URL Base:** https://eduhawkwebsite.vercel.app  
**Audit Status Standards:** PASS | FAIL | PARTIAL | BLOCKED | NOT TESTED  

---

## Audit Methodology & Inspection Protocol
Every individual page entry below is audited against the following criteria:
1. **Page Name & Live URL:** Verified canonical link.
2. **UI/UX Status:** Visual hierarchy, typography, colors, section spacing, cards, borders, buttons.
3. **Content Verification:** Factual alignment with original Markdown/HTML references in `E:\Downloads-pro\`.
4. **DCAP CMS Binding:** Accurate binding to Decap CMS JSON under `src/data/`.
5. **Image Verification:** Loading, aspect ratios, responsive scaling, object-fit.
6. **Button / Functionality:** CTA buttons, modals, dropdowns, navigation triggers.
7. **Responsive Design:** 1440px, 1366px, 768px, 390px, 360px viewport tests.
8. **SEO Status:** Page title, meta description, canonical tag, Open Graph, Twitter cards, Schema.org.
9. **Console / Network:** Zero unhandled exceptions or failed network asset requests.
